Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in cognitive theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 852 and the invariant I(A,B) = 858 mod 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 852 + O(1/n^858). Summing over all modes and applying the cognitive convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
